# FAQ

Source: `docs.entire.io/cli/faq.md`. The page contains exactly **two** questions, both under a **Checkpoints** heading.

## Checkpoints

### Why does Entire use commit trailers instead of Git notes?

Entire links commits to checkpoints using an `Entire-Checkpoint` trailer that points to checkpoint metadata in the `entire/checkpoints/v1` branch. Commit trailers survive common Git workflows better than metadata attached only to a commit SHA.

Git notes have limitations:

- They are not pushed by normal Git pushes unless note refs are configured separately.
- They are attached to commit SHAs, which change during rebases, amends, and cherry-picks.
- Hosted platforms do not consistently surface them in review workflows.

By embedding the checkpoint ID in the commit message itself, Entire maintains a reliable link that persists through rebasing, amending, cherry-picking, hosted merge flows, and across different Git clients.

### Why doesn't Entire provide durable line-level AI attribution?

Entire focuses on preserving session and commit provenance rather than permanent per-line ownership. It can answer which session created a commit and which prompts led to the changes, but line-level attribution is fragile during normal workflows: lines shift, split, merge with nearby edits, or get rewritten later.

Line tracking becomes unreliable when commits are rebased, amended, cherry-picked, or squashed; when conflicts are resolved manually; when merges happen in hosted UIs; or when human edits are mixed into commits.

Rather than implementing complex repair logic everywhere, Entire prioritizes three things:

1. Preserving full session transcripts.
2. Linking commits to stable checkpoint identifiers.
3. Maintaining commit-level provenance across standard Git workflows.

---

# Glossary

Source: `docs.entire.io/glossary.md` — 29 terms.

| Term | Definition |
|---|---|
| **Agent** | An AI coding assistant connected to Entire, such as Claude Code, Codex, Cursor, Factory, Gemini CLI, GitHub Copilot CLI, OpenCode, or Pi. |
| **Agent run** | One execution of an agent inside a session, where Entire compares work before each run with agent changes for line attribution accuracy. |
| **Checkpoint** | A save point for work that Entire creates around agent activity and links to Git commits. |
| **Checkpoint ID** | A 12-character hexadecimal identifier for a checkpoint, such as `8a513f56ed70`. |
| **Checkpoint linking** | The connection between a Git commit and the Entire checkpoint that captured the session context behind it. |
| **Checkpoint remote** | A Git remote used to store checkpoint metadata separately from your code remote. |
| **Checkpoints branch** | The permanent Git branch named `entire/checkpoints/v1` where Entire stores checkpoint and session metadata. |
| **Commit trailer** | Structured metadata appended to a Git commit message, such as `Entire-Checkpoint` and `Entire-Attribution`. |
| **Committed checkpoint** | A checkpoint permanently linked to a Git commit and stored on the checkpoints branch. |
| **Dispatch** | A markdown summary of recent agent work across repositories, branches, and time windows. |
| **Entire CLI** | The command line tool that records sessions, creates checkpoints, links commits, and syncs metadata. |
| **Entire.io** | The web application for browsing repositories, sessions, checkpoints, dispatches, and agent activity. |
| **Entire Repository** | A Git repository stored in EntireDB, Entire's distributed Git database. |
| **Entire-Attribution** | A commit trailer that records how much of a commit came from the agent versus a human. |
| **Entire-Checkpoint** | A commit trailer that records the checkpoint ID associated with a commit. |
| **Line attribution** | The calculation of how many changed lines in a commit were written by the agent versus a human. |
| **Metadata** | Structured data Entire records about sessions and checkpoints, including timestamps, agent type, token usage, and attribution. |
| **Mirror** | A synced, read-optimized copy of a GitHub repository hosted on Entire's Git network in a chosen region. |
| **Nested session** | A child session created when an agent spawns another agent or sub-agent during the same body of work. |
| **Rewind** | Restoring the worktree to an earlier checkpoint during an active session. |
| **Semantic Search** | Search indexed Entire history by phrase, meaning, or agent context to find related checkpoints, sessions, and commits. |
| **Session** | A complete interaction with an AI coding agent from start to finish, including prompts, responses, code changes, and metadata. |
| **Session branch** | A Git branch used to store captured session data without adding commits to your working branch. |
| **Session context** | The prompts, responses, tool activity, code changes, and metadata that explain what happened during a session. |
| **Shadow branch** | A temporary local branch used to store checkpoints during active sessions, named like `entire/&lt;sessionID&gt;-&lt;worktreeID&gt;`. |
| **Skill** | A reusable workflow that helps an agent use Entire history, checkpoints, and session context for a specific task. |
| **Token usage** | Counts of input tokens, output tokens, cache creation tokens, cache reads, and API calls recorded for a checkpoint or session. |
| **Worktree ID** | An opaque identifier Entire uses as part of a shadow branch name to distinguish active worktrees. |

# Discrepancy summary (docs vs installed 0.10.2)

Ranked by how likely they are to bite someone.

| # | Command | Discrepancy | Severity |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `review` | Docs claim `--timeout` defaults to `10m0s` with `0` to disable. Binary: **no default** — reviewers run unbounded; only the judge has an internal 20m default | High — docs imply reviews are capped when they are not |
| 2 | `login` | Docs describe device-code flow as the default and list zero flags. Binary defaults to **browser redirect**, with `--device` to opt into device-code, plus `--server` (default `https://auth.entire.io`) | High |
| 3 | `logout` | Docs describe it as a local token deletion, no flags. Binary revokes the session **server-side** and adds `--everywhere` and `--all-contexts`, plus context-promotion behavior | High |
| 4 | `review` | `--target REF` and `--cleanup-worktree` absent from docs; `--target` changes what gets reviewed | Medium-high |
| 5 | all | Global `--context name` flag exists on every command; documented on none of the 16 pages | Medium |
| 6 | `repo` | Docs omit `clone`, `visibility` (`get`/`set`), `mirror get`, `mirror list`, `mirror use`, plus `--json`/`--object-format` on `create` and all pagination/filter/sort flags | Medium |
| 7 | `labs` | Docs list only `review`. Binary lists 9 workflows including `tokens`, `blame`, `why`, `experts` | Medium |
| 8 | `session` | Docs omit `--transcript` on `info`/`current`, and all flags on `attach` (`-a/--agent`, `-f/--force`, `--review`, `--skills`) and `adopt` (`--force`, `--from`, `--yes`) | Medium |
| 9 | `import` | Docs put `--dry-run`/`--path`/`--session` on `entire import`; binary puts them on the agent subcommands only. `entire import --dry-run` will fail | Medium |
| 10 | `search` | Docs omit `--compact` (implies `--json`, agent-oriented shape) | Low-medium |
| 11 | `status` | Docs omit `--json` | Low |
| 12 | `version` | Docs sample shows `Entire CLI v1.2.3 (abc1234)`. Binary prints `Entire CLI 0.10.2` — no `v`, no commit hash | Low (matters if parsed) |
| 13 | `investigate` | Docs omit `--force` on `clean`; present `--issue-link` as an "input" rather than a flag | Low |
| 14 | n/a | `entire resume` is **not** a command (`unknown command "resume"`), despite the docs page living at `/cli-reference/resume.md`. The command is `entire session resume` | Low (naming trap) |

Two pages matched the binary exactly with zero discrepancies: **`recap`** and **`why`**.

One caveat on completeness: the docs pages for `repo` and `sessions` were returned by the fetcher in condensed form, so a handful of prose notes on those two pages may not be reproduced word-for-word. Every flag, default, and subcommand in this reference is verified directly against the installed binary, which is the authoritative source per your instruction. The `import` page also self-labels as "CLI 0.7.8", so it is two minor versions stale.</result>

**Captured:** 2026-08-21 from the locally installed binary
**Binary:** `/opt/homebrew/bin/entire`

```
Entire CLI 0.10.2
Go version: go1.26.6
OS/Arch: darwin/arm64
```
**Repo under test:** `gh/hxrshx/ehl-bayern-codewerk` (auto-detected from origin)

Everything below is copied verbatim from `--help` / `agent-help` output. No flag in this document was inferred.

`entire trail --help` works, but `entire agent-help trail` returns:

```
`trail` is unavailable: trails are not enabled for this repo
```

This is why `trail` does not appear in root help for this repository.

### 1.3 Confirmed NOT to exist as top-level commands

`entire attach`, `entire resume`, and `entire rewind` all fail with `Error: Invalid usage: unknown command "&lt;name&gt;" for "entire"`. Attach and resume exist only as `entire session attach` / `entire session resume` (and `entire trail resume`).

## 2. Global flags

Present on the root command and inherited by every subcommand:

| flag | arg | default | description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `--context` | `name` | — | Act as this saved login name for this command only, instead of the active context (entire auth contexts lists them) |
| `-h, --help` | — | — | help for entire |
| `-v, --version` | — | — | version for entire |

`--repo string` is an additional inherited flag on the whole `trail` subtree only: *Target repository as forge/owner/repo (e.g. gh/acme/app) or a clone URL; defaults to the origin remote*.

### Environment variables named in help output

| var | where documented | effect |
|---|---|---|
| `ACCESSIBLE` | root help | Set to any value to enable accessibility mode (simpler text prompts instead of interactive TUI) |
| `ENTIRE_TOKEN` | `auth token` | Used verbatim as the bearer when set |
| `ENTIRE_CONTEXT` | `auth use` | Selects the login context for one shell / one git operation |
| `ENTIRE_API_BASE_URL` | `auth use` | Host for activity/search/dispatch |
| `ENTIRE_LOG_LEVEL` | `doctor trace` | Set to `DEBUG` to emit hook traces |
| `ENTIRE_PLUGIN_DIR` | `plugin` | Overrides the managed plugin dir (`$ENTIRE_PLUGIN_DIR/bin`) |
| `ENTIRE_PLUGIN_INDEX_URL` | `plugin *` | Default plugin index URL, overridden by `--index` |
| `XDG_DATA_HOME` | `plugin` | `$XDG_DATA_HOME/entire/plugins/bin` |
| `LOCALAPPDATA` | `plugin` | `%LOCALAPPDATA%\entire\plugins\bin` (Windows) |
| `CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR` | `.claude/settings.json` (not CLI) | Used by this repo's local EHL hook |

## 3. Flags by command

Every table below is copied from `entire &lt;cmd&gt; --help`. `--context name` is omitted from each table since it is inherited everywhere (see §2). `-h, --help` is likewise present on every single command and is only listed where a command has no other flags.

### 3.1 Setup

**`entire activity`** — `entire activity [flags]`

| flag | arg | default | description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `--commits` | — | false | Show recent commits instead of recent sessions |

**`entire agent`** — `entire agent [flags]` / `entire agent [command]` — only `-h, --help`.

**`entire agent add &lt;agent-name&gt;`**

| flag | arg | default | description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `--agent-help-skill` | — | false | Install the stable Entire agent-help skill (points agents at `entire agent-help`) |
| `--force` | — | false | Reinstall hooks even if already present |
| `--search-skill` | — | false | Install the optional Entire search skill |

**`entire agent list`** — only `-h, --help`.

**`entire agent remove &lt;agent-name&gt;`** — only `-h, --help`.

**`entire agent-help [command...]`**

| flag | arg | default | description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `--json` | — | false | Emit structured JSON instead of text |

**`entire clean`**

| flag | arg | default | description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `-a, --all` | — | false | Clean all session data across the repository |
| `-d, --dry-run` | — | false | Preview what would be deleted without deleting |
| `-f, --force` | — | false | Skip confirmation prompt and override active session guard |
| `--session` | `string` | — | Clean a specific session by ID |

**`entire configure`**

| flag | arg | default | description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `--absolute-git-hook-path` | — | false | Embed full binary path in git hooks (for GUI git clients that don't source shell profiles) |
| `--checkpoint-backend` | `string` | — | Checkpoint storage backend: refs (one git ref per checkpoint; recommended) or branch (shared entire/checkpoints/v1 branch) |
| `--checkpoint-remote` | `string` | — | Checkpoint remote in provider:owner/repo format (e.g., github:org/checkpoints-repo) |
| `-f, --force` | — | false | Reinstall the Entire git hook |
| `--local` | — | false | Write settings to `.entire/settings.local.json` instead of `.entire/settings.json` |
| `--project` | — | false | Write settings to `.entire/settings.json` even if it already exists |
| `--skip-push-sessions` | — | false | Disable automatic pushing of session logs on git push |
| `--summarize-model` | `string` | — | Set the model hint used by explain --generate |
| `--summarize-provider` | `string` | — | Set the provider used by explain --generate (e.g., claude-code, codex, gemini, pi, cursor, copilot-cli) |
| `--summarize-timeout-seconds` | `int` | — | Set the hard deadline (seconds) for explain --generate summary generation. 0 clears (falls back to 5m default). |
| `--telemetry` | — | `true` | Enable anonymous usage analytics (default true) |

**`entire disable`**

| flag | arg | default | description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `--force` | — | false | Skip confirmation prompt (use with --uninstall) |
| `--local` | — | false | Update `.entire/settings.local.json` (the default) instead of `.entire/settings.json` |
| `--project` | — | false | Update `.entire/settings.json` instead of `.entire/settings.local.json` |
| `--uninstall` | — | false | Completely remove Entire from this repository |

**`entire enable`**

| flag | arg | default | description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `--absolute-git-hook-path` | — | false | Embed full binary path in git hooks (for GUI git clients that don't source shell profiles) |
| `--agent` | `string` | — | Agent to set up hooks for (e.g., claude-code, codex, copilot-cli, cursor, factoryai-droid, gemini, opencode, pi; external agents on $PATH are also available). Enables non-interactive mode. |
| `--agent-help-skill` | — | false | Install the stable Entire agent-help skill (points agents at `entire agent-help`) for selected agent(s) |
| `--checkpoint-backend` | `string` | — | Checkpoint storage backend: refs (one git ref per checkpoint; recommended) or branch (shared entire/checkpoints/v1 branch) |
| `--checkpoint-remote` | `string` | — | Checkpoint remote in provider:owner/repo format (e.g., github:org/checkpoints-repo) |
| `-f, --force` | — | false | Force reinstall hooks (removes existing Entire hooks first) |
| `--import-history` | — | false | During first-time setup, import the selected agents' existing session history (last 30 days) without prompting |
| `--init-repo` | — | false | If not a git repo, initialize one non-interactively |
| `--initial-commit-message` | `string` | — | Commit message for the initial commit when bootstrapping a new repo |
| `--local` | — | false | Write settings to `.entire/settings.local.json` instead of `.entire/settings.json` |
| `--no-github` | — | false | Initialize local git repo only; skip creating a GitHub remote |
| `--no-init-repo` | — | false | If not a git repo, exit instead of prompting to initialize one |
| `--project` | — | false | Write settings to `.entire/settings.json` even if it already exists |
| `--push` | — | false | When bootstrapping a new repo, push the initial commit to the created GitHub remote (implies creating the remote; without it the repo is created but not pushed) |
| `--repo-name` | `string` | — | GitHub repository name for the new repo (used when bootstrapping) |
| `--repo-owner` | `string` | — | GitHub user or organization login for the new repo |
| `--repo-visibility` | `string` | — | GitHub repository visibility: public, private, or internal |
| `--search-skill` | — | false | Install the optional Entire search skill for selected agent(s) |
| `--skip-initial-commit` | — | false | Don't create the initial commit when bootstrapping a new repo |
| `--skip-push-sessions` | — | false | Disable automatic pushing of session logs on git push |
| `--telemetry` | — | `true` | Enable anonymous usage analytics (default true) |
| `-y, --yes` | — | false | Accept all defaults without prompting (in a non-repo directory: init git, create private GitHub repo, commit, and push; then enable all agents and accept telemetry). Does not import existing agent history — see --import-history |

**`entire status`**

| flag | arg | default | description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `--detailed` | — | false | Show detailed status for each settings file |
| `--json` | — | false | Output as JSON |

### 3.5 Search, recap, dispatch

**`entire search [query]`** (and the identical `entire checkpoint search`)

| flag | arg | default | description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `--all-repos` | — | false | Search all accessible repos instead of just the current one |
| `--author` | `string` | — | Filter by author name |
| `--branch` | `string` | — | Filter by branch name |
| `--case-sensitive` | — | false | Case-sensitive code search (only with --code) |
| `--code` | — | false | Search code content across repositories |
| `--compact` | — | false | Trimmed JSON output for agents: id, repo, files touched, score, match snippet, and a truncated title instead of the full prompt (implies --json) |
| `--date` | `string` | — | Filter by time period (week or month) |
| `--json` | — | false | Output as JSON |
| `--limit` | `int` | `10` | Maximum number of results (per page for checkpoint search, total for --code) (default 10) |
| `--page` | `int` | `1` | Page number (1-based) (default 1) |
| `--repo` | `strings` | — | Filter by repository (gh/owner/repo, et/proj/repo, owner/repo, ULID, or *); repeatable and comma-separated for multiple repos |

Inline query filters supported: `author:&lt;name&gt;`, `date:&lt;week|month&gt;`, `branch:&lt;name&gt;`, `repo:&lt;owner/name&gt;`, `repo:*`.

**`entire recap`**

| flag | arg | default | description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `--90` | — | false | Rolling 90 days |
| `--agent` | `string` | `"all"` | Agent id to show, or all (default "all") |
| `--color` | `string` | `"auto"` | Color output: auto, always, or never (default "auto") |
| `--day` | — | default | Today only (default) |
| `--insecure-http-auth` | — | false | Allow plain-HTTP auth (local dev only) |
| `--month` | — | false | This calendar month |
| `--static` | — | false | Print static output instead of opening the interactive recap |
| `--view` | `string` | `"both"` | Which columns to show: you, team, or both (default "both") |
| `--week` | — | false | Last 7 days |

**`entire dispatch`**

| flag | arg | default | description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `--agent` | `string` | — | local text-generation agent (requires --local) |
| `--all-branches` | — | false | include every existing local branch (--local only; renamed or deleted branches are skipped) |
| `--local` | — | false | generate via the locally-installed agent CLI instead of the Entire server |
| `--repos` | `strings` | — | cloud repo slugs, up to 5 (for example entireio/cli) |
| `--since` | `string` | `"7d"` | time window (Go duration, relative time, or ISO date) (default "7d") |
| `--until` | `string` | now | window end time (defaults to now) |
| `--voice` | `string` | — | voice preset name or literal description |

### 3.7 Control plane

**`entire api &lt;path&gt;`**

| flag | arg | default | description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `-F, --field` | `stringArray` | — | add a typed parameter in key=value format; true/false/null/numbers are converted (repeatable) |
| `-H, --header` | `stringArray` | — | add a request header in key:value format (repeatable) |
| `-i, --include` | — | false | print the response status line and headers too |
| `--input` | `string` | — | file with the request body ("-" for stdin) |
| `-j, --jurisdiction` | `string` | — | target a specific jurisdiction's cell (e.g. us, eu) instead of your home cell; implies --to cell |
| `-X, --method` | `string` | GET (POST when field/body given) | HTTP method (default GET, or POST when a field/body is given) |
| `-f, --raw-field` | `stringArray` | — | add a string parameter in key=value format (repeatable) |
| `--to` | `string` | `"core"` | which backend to call: core or cell (default "core") |

Path placeholders filled from the origin remote: `{owner}`, `{repo}`, `{repo_id}`.

**`entire repo list &lt;project&gt;`** — grouped flags exactly as printed:

*Navigation Flags*

| flag | arg | default | description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `--all` | — | false | Fetch every repository instead of the first 1000 (slower on large projects) |
| `--limit` | `int` | `0` | Fetch and show only the first N repositories (0 uses the default fetch budget) |
| `--page-size` | `int` | — | Fetch a single page of at most N entries (1-500; the server may cap N further) and print the resume cursor |
| `--page-token` | `string` | — | Fetch the single page at this cursor (from a previous run's nextPageToken) |

*Formatting Flags*

| flag | arg | default | description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `--json` | — | false | Output raw JSON instead of a table |
| `--no-pager` | — | false | Print directly to stdout instead of a pager for long output |

**`entire repo mirror collaborators list &lt;github-url&gt; [cluster-host]`** — `--json`. Cluster-host defaults to `aws-us-east-2.entire.io`.

**`entire repo mirror create [github-url] [cluster-host]`**

| flag | arg | default | description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `--no-wait` | — | false | Return once the placement is registered, without waiting for the initial clone |
| `--wait-timeout` | `duration` | `30m0s` | How long to wait for the initial clone to finish (default 30m0s) |

**`entire repo mirror get &lt;mirror&gt;`** — `--json`.

**`entire repo mirror list`** — grouped flags exactly as printed:

*Navigation Flags*

| flag | arg | default | description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `--all` | — | false | Fetch the complete directory instead of the first 1000 entries (slower on large orgs) |
| `--limit` | `int` | `0` | Show at most N rows, applied after the local filters and sort (0 shows all fetched) |
| `--page-size` | `int` | — | Fetch a single page of at most N entries and print the resume cursor |
| `--page-token` | `string` | — | Fetch the single page at this cursor (from a previous run's nextPageToken) |

*Filtering &amp; Sorting Flags* — "Applied only to the fetched rows; combine with --all to filter/sort the complete mirror list."

| flag | arg | default | description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `--access` | `string` | — | Filter by exact ACCESS (candidates only: read/write/admin) |
| `--available` | — | false | Keep only GitHub repos you could onboard as mirrors (drops existing mirrors) |
| `--cluster` | `string` | — | Keep only repos mirrored on this cluster, by slug or public host (drops onboardable candidates) |
| `--mirrored` | — | false | Keep only repos already mirrored (drops onboardable candidates) |
| `--name` | `string` | — | Filter by owner/repo substring, matching the NAME column (case-insensitive) |
| `--owner` | `string` | — | Filter by upstream owner login |
| `--private` | — | all | Filter by visibility: --private for private only, --private=false for public only (omit for all) |
| `--sort` | `string` | name ascending | Sort by column key (e.g. name, clusters; prefix '-' for descending). Default: name ascending |
| `--status` | `string` | — | Filter by exact STATUS (mirrors: ready/processing/failed/suspended, matching any of a repo's placements; candidates: available/owner-only) |

*Formatting Flags* — `--json`, `--no-pager` (as above).

**`entire repo mirror remove &lt;github-url&gt; [cluster-host]`** — only `-h, --help`.

**`entire repo mirror use [github-url] [cluster-host]`**

| flag | arg | default | description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `--cluster` | `string` | — | Cluster host to use when the repo is mirrored on several (same as [cluster-host]) |
| `--remote` | `string` | `"origin"` | Git remote to point at the mirror (default "origin") |
| `--upstream` | `string` | `"upstream"` | Remote to preserve the replaced URL under; empty to discard it (default "upstream") |

**`entire repo visibility get &lt;repo&gt;`** — `--json`, `--project string`.
**`entire repo visibility set &lt;repo&gt; &lt;public|private&gt;`** — `--json`, `--project string`.

| `--force` | — | false | Replace an existing entry with the same name |

| `--no-deps` | — | false | Do not install declared dependencies |
| `--pin` | `string` | — | Install exactly this tag and skip it during 'plugin upgrade' |

**`entire experts [scope-or-query]`**

| flag | arg | default | description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `--branch` | `string` | — | Branch to inspect |
| `--json` | — | false | Print JSON |
| `--limit` | `int` | `8` | Maximum profiles to return (1–20; values above 20 are clamped) (default 8) |
| `--repo` | `string` | — | Repository as owner/repo |
| `--staged` | — | false | Use staged file paths as scopes |
| `--tui` | — | false | Browse provenance in an interactive viewer (TTY only) |

**`entire tokens`** — only `-h, --help`.

| flag | arg | default | description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `--all` | — | false | Analyze all committed checkpoints |

### 3.10 Trail (hidden; gated per repo)

Inherited on the whole subtree: `--repo string` (Target repository as forge/owner/repo (e.g. gh/acme/app) or a clone URL; defaults to the origin remote).

**`entire trail`** — `--repo string` (declared here as a local flag), `-h, --help`.

| `--worktree` | — | false | Check out the trail branch in a worktree under `.entire/worktrees` instead of switching this checkout |

**`entire trail comment`** (parent) — `--branch string`, `--trail string` (Trail number, id, or branch (defaults to the current branch)); both inherited by all comment subcommands.

## 4. Verbatim `entire agent-help` contract text

```
Entire's CLI is the source of truth for its own usage. Do not guess flags or
subcommands — read them from this command. You are already inside the repo:
entire auto-detects it from the git origin remote, so never ask the user for the
repo name. Pass --repo only to target a DIFFERENT repo.

Current repo: gh/hxrshx/ehl-bayern-codewerk  (auto-detected from origin; pass --repo only for a DIFFERENT repo)

When to use entire — the commands that bear on the work:
  checkpoint  Inspect and search checkpoints
                task-driven
  search      Search checkpoints, commits, and sessions using semantic and keyword matching
                read-only
  session     Manage agent sessions tracked by Entire
                read-only except: adopt, attach, resume, stop
  status      Show Entire status
                read-only
  why         Show why a line exists
                read-only

Also available (entire agent-help &lt;command&gt; for any of these):
  read-only, safe to run:
    activity · labs · recap · version
  when the task needs them:
    api · dispatch · doctor
  the user's — suggest, don't run:
    agent · auth · clean · configure · disable · enable · grant
    login · logout · org · plugin · project · repo

Drill in for exact, currently-installed flags:  entire agent-help &lt;command&gt;  (e.g. entire agent-help checkpoint)
Add --json for structured output.
```

### 4.1 Safety classification the binary assigns

`entire agent-help &lt;cmd&gt;` appends a classification line under `Subcommands:`. These are the binary's own words:

| command | classification |
|---|---|
| `checkpoint` | `task-driven` |
| `doctor` | `task-driven` |
| `search` | `read-only` |
| `status` | `read-only` |
| `why` | `read-only` |
| `session` | `read-only except: adopt, attach, resume, stop` |
| `agent`, `auth`, `grant`, `org`, `plugin`, `project`, `repo` | `user-owned` |
| `activity`, `labs`, `recap`, `version` | listed under "read-only, safe to run" |
| `api`, `dispatch`, `doctor` | listed under "when the task needs them" |

### 4.2 The one prose block `agent-help` adds that `--help` does not

Only `entire agent-help api` carries a "When to use this" section. Verbatim:

```
When to use this:
LAST RESORT. Right in two cases: you are developing against Entire's own
APIs and want a raw response, or no first-class command covers your need.
Otherwise prefer the command built for the job (checkpoint, session, trail,
status, repo, …) — its output is stable, raw endpoints are not. If you are
reaching for this during ordinary work in a repo with Entire enabled, run
`entire agent-help` first; there is probably a command for it. When you do
need it, use this rather than hand-rolling curl — it attaches the right
bearer and dials the right host for you.
```

Every other `agent-help &lt;cmd&gt;` output is the command's long description + Flags + Inherited flags + the `Current repo:` line, restructured but not adding new flags. The `Flags:` blocks match `--help` exactly minus `-h, --help`.

### 4.3 Commands `agent-help` does NOT know about

These are real, working binary commands that `entire agent-help &lt;cmd&gt;` rejects with `Error: Invalid usage: unknown command "&lt;name&gt;"; run `entire agent-help` for the list of commands`:

`agent-help` (itself), `blame`, `experts`, `import` (and all 7 sources), `investigate` (and all 3 subcommands), `review`, `tokens` (and `tokens profile`).

Notably `why` and `blame` are both labs commands but only `why` is in the agent-help contract — `blame` is not.

`entire agent-help trail` returns a distinct message, not an error:
```
`trail` is unavailable: trails are not enabled for this repo
```

```json

  "enabled": true,
  "checkpoints": {
    "primary": {
      "type": "git-branch"
    }
  }
}
```

Note: the checkpoint backend is `git-branch`, which `entire configure --checkpoint-backend` describes as the non-recommended option ("refs (one git ref per checkpoint; recommended) or branch (shared entire/checkpoints/v1 branch)"). `entire doctor migrate-checkpoints` is the documented conversion path.

Entire's Claude Code integration therefore uses seven `entire hooks claude-code &lt;event&gt;` verbs: `pre-task`, `post-task`, `post-todo`, `session-start`, `session-end`, `stop`, `user-prompt-submit`. None of these are documented by `entire hooks --help`.

### 5.6 Git hooks

`ls -la .git/hooks/` (non-sample entries only):
```
-rwxr-xr-x@  1 pavinsp  staff   416 Aug 21 20:31 commit-msg
-rwxr-xr-x@  1 pavinsp  staff   215 Aug 21 20:31 post-commit
-rwxr-xr-x@  1 pavinsp  staff   210 Aug 21 20:31 post-rewrite
-rwxr-xr-x@  1 pavinsp  staff   213 Aug 21 20:31 pre-push
-rwxr-xr-x@  1 pavinsp  staff   150 Aug 21 20:31 prepare-commit-msg
```

All five mention entire. Verbatim:

**`.git/hooks/commit-msg`**
```sh
#!/bin/sh
# Entire CLI hooks
# Commit-msg hook: strip trailer if no user content (allows aborting empty commits)
if command -v entire &gt;/dev/null 2&gt;&amp;1; then entire hooks git commit-msg "$1" || true; else printf '%s\n' '[entire] Entire CLI is enabled but not installed or not on PATH. Skipping Entire Git hook; continuing. Installation guide: https://docs.entire.io/cli/installation#installation-methods' &gt;&amp;2 || :; fi
```

**`.git/hooks/post-commit`**
```sh
#!/bin/sh
# Entire CLI hooks
# Post-commit hook: condense session data if commit has Entire-Checkpoint trailer
if command -v entire &gt;/dev/null 2&gt;&amp;1; then entire hooks git post-commit 2&gt;/dev/null || true; else :; fi
```

**`.git/hooks/post-rewrite`**
```sh
#!/bin/sh
# Entire CLI hooks
# Post-rewrite hook: remap session linkage after amend/rebase rewrites
if command -v entire &gt;/dev/null 2&gt;&amp;1; then entire hooks git post-rewrite "$1" 2&gt;/dev/null || true; else :; fi
```

**`.git/hooks/pre-push`**
```sh
#!/bin/sh
# Entire CLI hooks
# Pre-push hook: push session logs alongside user's push
# $1 is the remote name (e.g., "origin")
if command -v entire &gt;/dev/null 2&gt;&amp;1; then entire hooks git pre-push "$1"; else :; fi
```

**`.git/hooks/prepare-commit-msg`**
```sh
#!/bin/sh
# Entire CLI hooks
if command -v entire &gt;/dev/null 2&gt;&amp;1; then entire hooks git prepare-commit-msg "$1" "$2" 2&gt;/dev/null || true; else :; fi
```

Five `entire hooks git &lt;event&gt;` verbs: `commit-msg`, `post-commit`, `post-rewrite`, `pre-push`, `prepare-commit-msg`.

**Discrepancy against `entire disable --uninstall`:** its help text lists four hooks it removes — "Git hooks (prepare-commit-msg, commit-msg, post-commit, pre-push)". `post-rewrite` is installed on disk but is **not** named in that list.

```
61b09cf18a72281186ecd1af44f6fd37a6d967a9	refs/entire/checkpoints/7P/01M0JS0N7Q8EVW3C5EBCM4XW7P
2db6267510b519cd844eebcd51d175d1ebe55962	refs/heads/docs/entire-setup-guide
3e2c26dfa3b66186c040dd1147331d163f8d8eeb	refs/heads/entire/checkpoints/v1
```

`git remote -v`: `origin  https://github.com/hxrshx/ehl-bayern-codewerk.git` (fetch and push).

Both storage layouts are present simultaneously: the `git-branch` backend at `refs/heads/entire/checkpoints/v1` (configured primary) and one stray `git-refs` checkpoint at `refs/entire/checkpoints/7P/01M0JS0N7Q8EVW3C5EBCM4XW7P` — matching the oldest checkpoint `01M0JS0N7Q8EVW3C5EBCM4XW7P` in `checkpoint list`, the only one with a ULID-style ID. The local `refs/heads/entire/checkpoints/v1` (`03d6e85…`) is ahead of `origin` (`3e2c26d…`), which is the "1 checkpoint not yet on origin" that `entire status` reports.

## 7. DISCREPANCIES

### 7.1 Docs-only — a cli-reference page exists but the binary has no such top-level command

| docs page | reality in 0.10.2 |
|---|---|
| `attach` | **Not a command.** `entire attach` → `Error: Invalid usage: unknown command "attach" for "entire"`. Exists only as `entire session attach &lt;session-id&gt;`. |
| `resume` | **Not a command.** `entire resume` → `unknown command "resume"`. Exists only as `entire session resume [branch]` and `entire trail resume [&lt;trail&gt;]`. |
| `global-flags` | Not a command at all — a concept page. The binary's only true global flag is `--context name` (plus root-only `-h/--help`, `-v/--version`). `--repo string` is subtree-global to `trail` only. |
| `sessions` | Resolves, but only as an **alias** of `session`. `entire sessions --help` prints the `session` help. |

### 7.2 Binary-only — command exists and works but has no page in the supplied docs list

| command | visible in `entire --help`? | notes |
|---|---|---|
| `api` | yes (Control Plane) | Fully documented in-binary; `agent-help api` calls it "LAST RESORT" |
| `grant` | yes (Control Plane) | 3 subcommands × 3 verbs = 9 leaf commands |
| `org` | yes (Control Plane) | 4 subcommands |
| `project` | yes (Control Plane) | 4 subcommands |
| `plugin` | yes (Entire Setup) | 9 subcommands + `index update` |
| `session` | yes | docs list has `sessions` (the alias) instead |
| `experts` | **no** — hidden; advertised by `labs` | |
| `tokens` / `tokens profile` | **no** — hidden; advertised by `labs` | |
| `trail` (+ 13 subcommands, 16 leaf grandchildren) | **no** — hidden, repo-gated | Largest undocumented surface: ~30 commands |
| `hooks` | **no** — hidden | Self-described internal. 12 observed verbs: `git {commit-msg,post-commit,post-rewrite,pre-push,prepare-commit-msg}` and `claude-code {pre-task,post-task,post-todo,session-start,session-end,stop,user-prompt-submit}`. None are listed by `entire hooks --help`. |
| `completion` | **no** — hidden | Standard Cobra; `bash`/`fish`/`powershell`/`zsh` |

### 7.3 Commands hidden from root help but present in docs

`blame`, `import`, `investigate`, `review`, `why` all have docs pages and all work, but **none appear in `entire --help`**. They are reachable only via `entire labs` or by knowing the name. Anyone building a reference from `entire --help` alone would omit five documented commands.

### 7.4 Internal inconsistencies inside the binary

1. **`agent-help` coverage gap.** `agent-help` is presented as "the source of truth for its own usage" and "always matches the installed CLI", but it rejects 13 working commands: `agent-help`, `blame`, `experts`, `import` (+7 sources), `investigate` (+3 subs), `review`, `tokens` (+`profile`). Of the labs commands it advertises in its own contract, it covers `why` but not `blame` — even though `labs` lists them adjacently.
2. **`entire status --json` undercounts sessions.** Human view: 3 active sessions. JSON: `"active_sessions"` array of length 1. Same invocation, same moment.
3. **`entire disable --uninstall` help omits `post-rewrite`.** It names four git hooks it removes; five are installed.
4. **`labs` advertises `entire tokens` twice under different shapes** — as `entire tokens` ("Analyze experimental token usage diagnostics") and `entire tokens profile` — but `entire tokens` itself has no flags beyond `-h` and only exists to host `profile`.
5. **`checkpoint search` and `search` are byte-identical** in long description, examples, and flags. `checkpoint search` is a duplicate registration.
6. **`login`/`logout` are exact duplicates** of `auth login`/`auth logout` (identical long descriptions and flag sets), registered at both levels.
7. **Two different `--repo` meanings.** Subtree-global on `trail` it means "target repository as forge/owner/repo…"; on `trail resume` a local `--repo` means "Expected GitHub repository (owner/name); fails if the current checkout points elsewhere". `checkpoint explain --repo` and `search --repo` are a third and fourth distinct meaning — `search --repo` is a `strings` filter, and `checkpoint search`'s own help explicitly warns the two are "unrelated".
8. **Malformed `Examples:` rendering in `agent-help`.** The first example line is concatenated onto the `Examples:` header (e.g. `Examples:\nentire why src/auth.go:42\n  entire why src/auth.go:42 --json`) while subsequent lines keep their two-space indent. Cosmetic, but it means agent-help output is not cleanly parseable by indentation.

### 7.5 Config-level discrepancy in this repo

`.entire/settings.json` pins `checkpoints.primary.type = "git-branch"`, which the binary's own `--checkpoint-backend` help describes as the alternative to the **recommended** `refs` backend. Yet `refs/entire/checkpoints/7P/01M0JS0N7Q8EVW3C5EBCM4XW7P` exists locally and on origin, so the repo currently holds checkpoints in both layouts. `entire doctor` reports `✓ Metadata branches: OK` and does not flag the split.

---

## 8. Capture method

- All output taken from `/opt/homebrew/bin/entire` 0.10.2 on darwin/arm64, run from the repo root.
- The tree was walked breadth-first: `--help` on every command, then the `Available Commands:` block of each result fed back as the next level, to fixpoint. Depth 4 was reached (`repo mirror collaborators list`) and confirmed terminal.
- Hidden commands were found three ways: reading `entire labs`, reading the hook command strings in `.claude/settings.json` and `.git/hooks/*`, and name-probing (`trail`, `completion`, `rewind`, `attach`, `resume`, aliases).
- **Caveat on shell splitting:** the default shell here is zsh, which does not word-split unquoted parameters. An initial pass using `entire $c --help` in zsh silently sent `"agent add"` as one argument and produced false "no subcommands" results for the entire second level. Every probe was re-run under `/bin/bash` with `grep -c '^Error:'` verification returning 0 before any table in this document was written.
- No mutating command was run. Executed: `--help`/`agent-help` across the tree, plus `status`, `status --detailed`, `status --json`, `version`, `doctor`, `checkpoint list --no-pager`, `session list`, `activity`, `agent list`, `auth status`. `entire doctor` is interactive-fix-capable but reported no issues and prompted for nothing.
- `.entire/metadata/` was not enumerated: this repo's `.claude/settings.json` denies reading it.</result>

</task-notification>
